I don't know, it just moves on to the mortgage, or a client renewal. I don't know. I guess... not really. I haven't slept well in so long that... I can't really say. I just don't know if it would change anything.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your worrying would be less if you were sleeping well?\n\nPatient: I mean... you just asked me that. I still don't know. I can't really say if it would make a difference.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your worrying would stop if you fell asleep quickly and slept through the night?\n\nPatient: I...
